# Full test audit review

The cleanup on `test/full-test-audit` is broadly sound after correcting the coverage gaps below. Keep the consolidations and removal of copied implementations, static-value assertions and duplicate examples. The original cleanup also removed distinct behavior and failure paths; those are restored in production-facing tests, often as extra rows or assertions in retained cases. No production implementation changed.

The comparison covers all 2,144 originally changed files against `da0654f88bbcc7e0dba708738bcbb5a013edef70`, through the reviewed commit `459c40511dd6d3bdc0bd5c65871c95fee38c0b0b` and the audit corrections in this branch. The original commit modified 1,698 files and deleted 446, adding 64,803 lines and removing 174,965. All 2,144 paths have exactly one review entry, with no omissions or duplicate ownership. Test-declaration counts in the file review describe the original branch change, before these corrections and before parameterized expansion.

The [case evidence](/tmp/daintree-audit/full-review.json) contains removed and added case records, original assertion evidence where available, relocation candidates and coverage destinations. A matching title or body is evidence for a consolidation, not by itself proof that a replacement exercises the same production subject; fixture scope, production paths and stronger retained cases were also checked.

## Coverage restored

| Area | Corrected behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Security and privacy | Preserve IPC error-detail allowlists, no-follow/nonblocking file-open flags, JSON-doubled WSL redaction, backslash traversal rejection, updater private-address refusal, ciphertext backend refusal, plugin SDK entry restrictions and MCP schema rejection paths. Restore Sentry hook scrubbing and appropriate grant revocation or exclusion. |
| Process and terminal lifecycle | Preserve archive drain handoff and restart, readiness gates, remote transport leases and unsubscribe, release pause tokens after either emitter throws, clean up outstanding capture leases, rearm targeted trimming, respond immediately to critical worker memory, and report forced resumes correctly. Restore terminal exit/null-resize/error routing, plain shell exit mode and the no-duplicate-stdin launch invariant. |
| Cancellation and state | Restore successful voice assembly superseded by stop or another start, search-cache expiry, broadcast cancellation versus settlement, store re-read after eviction, intermittent subscriber recovery, and zero detune forwarding. Reset fixture overrides so restored races cannot influence later tests. |
| UI and plugin contracts | Preserve all onboarding action routes without prematurely completing the checklist, Option plus Right and extra-modifier handling, simultaneous query/generated-file filtering, independent stable editor compartments, checkbox event isolation, plugin dockability changes, and GitHub fork metadata boundaries. |
| Package builds | Verify real tour/plugin-ui externalization and unserved-subpath refusal, alongside React. Exercise the live watcher handle rather than checking its constructor name. Restore action-running behavior and compatibility with a host lacking that field. |
| Test harness typing | Correct the consolidated terminal context-menu mock to include its optional branch field and the skeleton-theme store mock to admit malformed persisted values. Use a complete plugin panel fixture. Test resolver results structurally rather than requiring object alias identity. |

## Additional removals

The remaining local title resolver and window bootstrap/action simulator tested copied implementations. They are replaced with direct tests of the production sanitizer and workspace/path resolvers. The bystander test measured 120 ms while claiming to detect cleanup of a 300 ms timeout and imposed a machine-dependent stall threshold; its deterministic fake-timer replacement already verifies timer ownership and cleanup. Fixed compartment-count assertions stay removed, while compartment independence and stability are restored.

Other large deletions remain justified: test-only implementations of drag/drop, diagnostic redaction, command validation, updater metadata validation and panel wrappers; boilerplate JSON examples; declaration/default snapshots; duplicate error or lifecycle examples; and satellite suites whose actual assertions survive in consolidated files. Literal expected values are retained when they verify computed output, routing, boundaries or user behavior.

## CI and documentation

The separate `perf-liveness` job preserves the existing liveness guard, excludes it from ordinary shards, and participates in `ci-ok`. Its Linux setup and Node environment match the ordinary test jobs. Vitest's installed CLI appends the new exclusion to the configured exclusions. The guard remains a liveness check, with its existing cost/packaging exclusions; it establishes no performance improvement.

Documentation and lesson references point to the retained consolidated suites. The no-assertion energy experiment remains available through its documented historical restore command, whose Git object exists. The PTY fabric document now describes retained singleton-path coverage without claiming every old test is unchanged.

## Validation

`npm run check` passed with exit 0: typechecking, generated-code and registry guards, API surface, lint ratchet, E2E wait ratchet and repository-wide formatting. All 54 edited test files passed targeted verification, and `git diff --check` passed. The independent review has no unresolved findings. At the user's subsequent explicit request, the full Vitest unit suite also ran locally on macOS with four workers, including scenario liveness: 3,317 files and 72,086 tests passed, with 6 files and 47 tests skipped, exit 0. The run took 513.34 seconds and found no failures requiring further fixes. E2E was not run.

| Command scope | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Root package/shared corrections, 7 named files | 175 passed, exit 0 |
| Root IPC/PTY corrections, 12 named files | 370 passed, exit 0 |
| Electron main corrections, 12 named files | 511 passed, 1 skipped, exit 0 |
| Electron main schema/cipher/SDK corrections, 4 named files | 153 passed, exit 0 |
| Terminal IPC corrections, 5 named files | 255 passed, exit 0 |
| Component corrections, 4 named files | 94 passed, exit 0 |
| Renderer corrections, 6 named files with one revised-file rerun | 136 tests in the final six files passed; all commands exited 0 |
| GitHub PR and bystander corrections, 2 named files | 66 passed, exit 0 |
| Voice assembly/store eviction cross-review refinements, 2 named files | 69 passed, exit 0 |
| Final plugin panel/context-menu/resolver/voice refinements, 4 named files | 109 passed, exit 0 |
| Open-kind plugin panel fixture rerun, 1 named file | 13 passed, exit 0 |
| Skeleton theme consolidation fixture, 1 named file | 35 passed, exit 0 |
| Full unit suite, `npm test -- --maxWorkers=4`, including scenario liveness | 3,317 files and 72,086 tests passed; 6 files and 47 tests skipped; 513.34 seconds, exit 0 |
| First repository check | Exit 2: one incomplete new panel fixture and four excess branch-field errors in the branch's consolidated context-menu fixture; both corrected |
| Second repository check | Exit 2: the plugin fixture used the closed built-in union; corrected it to the open-kind TerminalInstance input accepted by isDockPanel |
| Third repository check | Exit 2: the merged skeleton-theme store mock inferred only string IDs; annotated its raw return as unknown so the malformed numeric-ID case typechecks |
| Final repository check | `npm run check` passed, exit 0; 1,128 lint warnings remain within the existing 2,975-warning baseline, with no baseline change |

An intermediate root test run failed because an attempted clone-URL helper restoration imported the helper from the wrong module. Coverage tracing then confirmed that the unchanged `gitRemoteUrl.test.ts` already covers those URL forms more extensively, so the redundant restoration was withdrawn. The corrected 12-file root batch passed all 370 tests. No assertion was weakened to hide a failure and no baseline was widened.

### src tests

- `src/__tests__/App.fileDropGuard.test.tsx` — keep cleanup. +0 / −13 lines. The removed already-prevented file drag is a representative duplicate of the retained internal-file drag handling; child bubbling and global navigation prevention remain exercised.
- `src/__tests__/PostHydrationListeners.test.tsx` — keep cleanup. +0 / −20 lines. The deferred gate-flip case proves the listener hooks stay dormant then each mount once; empty rendering and direct mount/perf-mark smoke assertions add no separate failure mode.
- `src/__tests__/adversarial-logic.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +0 / −90 lines. Repeated adversarial preset fixtures are covered by the actual preset merging and shared shell-sanitizer suites; retained direct production tests cover invalid IDs, duplicate precedence and non-string circular values.

### src clients

- `src/clients/__tests__/copyTreeClient.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +0 / −25 lines. Removed undefined-argument forwarding variants of thin IPC wrappers; defined optional names and actual result/error contracts remain exercised.
- `src/clients/__tests__/diffMediaClient.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +0 / −8 lines. Kept cleanup after comparing removed bodies with retained production behavior. Removed representative/default variants include sends no known versions on a first visit. Retained discriminating cases include revalidates a revisit and reuses the held data URLs for unchanged sides; replaces a side that changed and keeps the one that didn't; forgets a side that turned into an error. The full retained case inventory and relocation evidence are recorded in the linked case evidence.
- `src/clients/__tests__/filesClient.test.ts` — corrected during review. +0 / −45 lines. Restored search-cache TTL expiry because the remaining LRU/keying cases cannot fail when expiry is disabled. Kept deletion of FIFO and alternative explicit-limit variants dominated by retained LRU/keying tests.
- `src/clients/__tests__/globalEnvClient.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +0 / −21 lines. The set-during-get race exercises the same invalidation generation fence with a real write; the direct invalidation-only variant is redundant.
- `src/clients/__tests__/operationsClient.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +2 / −36 lines. Deleted crypto.randomUUID and transparent IPC wrapper smoke cases; there is no local UUID algorithm or conditional routing being exercised.
- `src/clients/__tests__/projectClient.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +0 / −71 lines. Kept cleanup after comparing removed bodies with retained production behavior. Removed representative/default variants include invalidateCurrentCache works directly without onSwitch; returns cached result for follow-on calls within TTL; isolates cache per projectId. Retained discriminating cases include coalesces concurrent getCurrent() calls into a single IPC call; returns cached result on subsequent calls after resolution; caches null results. The full retained case inventory and relocation evidence are recorded in the linked case evidence.
- `src/clients/__tests__/systemClient.openInEditor.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +0 / −43 lines. Consolidated production-path cases into `src/clients/__tests__/systemClient.test.ts`. Exact or same-title destinations were checked against removed bodies; duplicated file-local harness and imports were removed.
- `src/clients/__tests__/systemClient.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +40 / −0 lines. Retained added consolidation of production-path cases; fixture sharing preserves the independent behavior assertions and teardown. Relocation destinations from deleted sibling files were checked against the additions.
- `src/clients/__tests__/terminalClient.closedIdLeak.bench.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +0 / −74 lines. Deleted the hundreds-of-terminals heap/timing apparatus; the retained terminalClient test explicitly drops late MessagePort chunks after kill and checks revived IDs.
- `src/clients/__tests__/terminalClient.test.ts` — keep cleanup. +0 / −21 lines. The deleted unknown-ID tier event is transparent callback forwarding; MessagePort event transport and data-loss status routing remain covered.

### src components

- `src/components/Accessibility/__tests__/AccessibilityAnnouncer.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −74 lines. Declarations 23 → 15. The announcer still tests independent channels, duplicate-message clearing, stale callbacks, DOM delivery after ariaNotify throws, and StrictMode/timer cleanup. The deleted initial-render/one-message examples repeat those stronger delivery paths. Coverage: `src/store/__tests__/accessibilityAnnouncerStore.test.ts`.
- `src/components/ActionPalette/ActionPalette.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −32 lines. Declarations 52 → 49. Retained behavior includes does not render the empty message when a typed query has zero matches; shows the empty message when the registry exposes no eligible actions; keeps the sectioned body while a typed query's results are still catching up.
- `src/components/ActionPalette/ActionPaletteItem.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −61 lines. Declarations 39 → 34. Retained behavior includes renders disabled action with inline disabled reason; does not render disabled reason for disabled actions without a reason; does not render disabled reason for enabled actions with disabledReason.
- `src/components/AllClearOverlay/__tests__/AllClearOverlay.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −26 lines. Declarations 14 → 12. Retained behavior includes does not render before the callback fires; suppresses the overlay when the event carries shouldFlash: false; suppresses the overlay when prefers-reduced-motion is set.
- `src/components/Browser/__tests__/BrowserPane.webview.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −187 lines. Declarations 105 → 94. Blocked navigation, screenshot rejection/about:blank, stuck-load cleanup, and seed-only navigation remain covered by stronger lifecycle cases. The retained second-crash test requires exactly one notification carrying the second crash reason, which also proves the first crash stayed silent.
- `src/components/Browser/__tests__/BrowserPaneSkeleton.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −12 lines. Declarations 9 → 7. Retained behavior includes renders a polite status region that is never marked busy; accepts a custom label; pulses all placeholder shapes immediately — never the delayed gate (Suspense fallback).
- `src/components/Browser/__tests__/BrowserToolbar.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −37 lines. Declarations 94 → 90. Retained behavior includes calls onReload when submitting the same URL; calls onNavigate when submitting a different URL; calls onReload when display-format input normalizes to same URL.
- `src/components/Browser/__tests__/FindBar.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −7 lines. Declarations 8 → 7. Retained behavior includes input aria-describedby matches counter span id and counter has live-region attrs; counter span retains role=status before any query is entered (live region exists on mount); match-case button has aria-pressed reflecting matchCase state.
- `src/components/Browser/__tests__/browserUtils.test.ts` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −4 lines. Declarations 30 → 29. Retained behavior includes should normalize localhost URLs; should normalize 127.0.0.1 URLs; should handle IPv6 loopback hostname.
- `src/components/Commands/__tests__/CommandPicker.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −20 lines. Declarations 22 → 20. - `src/components/CopyTree/__tests__/CopyTreeRecentsPanel.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −8 lines. Declarations 29 → 28. The recents initialization smoke assertion repeats first-open hydration/fetch behavior, which remains covered by real menu-state tests.
- `src/components/Demo/__tests__/DemoCursor.test.tsx` — approved redundant/static-case pruning. +0 / −188 lines. Declarations 56 → 47. Retained behavior includes registers exec command listeners on mount; cleans up listeners on unmount; moveToSelector animates to element center.
